- **search-products**: Search Lazada Malaysia by keyword and return structured product results (title, price MYR, rating, reviews, seller, LazMall flag, discount, canonical URL) with optional filters for price range, location, shipping, and sort. Read-only. ([washingtoneimae-dot/agent](https://github.com/washingtoneimae-dot/agent/tree/HEAD/skills-library/browse-sh-skills/lazada_com_my-search-products-ancb86) · ★1)
